/**
   * Determines whether a variable name reference in a particular scope is a
   * global variable reference.
   *
   * @param name A variable name (e.g. "a")
   * @param s The scope in which the name is referenced
   * @return Whether the name reference is a global variable reference
   */
Method code:
191 |   private boolean isGlobalVarReference(String name, Scope s) {
192 |     Scope.Var v = s.getVar(name);
193 |     if (v == null && externsScope != null) {
194 |       v = externsScope.getVar(name);
195 |     }
196 |     return v != null && !v.isLocal();
197 |   }

===== M0010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 666-704
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name's longest prefix before the "prototype" property if the
     * name includes the "prototype" property. Does nothing otherwise.
     *
     * @param t The current node traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @return Whether the name was handled
     */
Method code:
666 |     boolean maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent,
667 |         String name) {
668 |       // We use a string-based approach instead of inspecting the parse tree
669 |       // to avoid complexities with object literals, possibly nested, beneath
670 |       // assignments.
671 | 
672 |       int numLevelsToRemove;
673 |       String prefix;
674 |       if (name.endsWith(".prototype")) {
675 |         numLevelsToRemove = 1;
676 |         prefix = name.substring(0, name.length() - 10);
677 |       } else {
678 |         int i = name.indexOf(".prototype.");
679 |         if (i == -1) {
680 |           return false;
681 |         }
682 |         prefix = name.substring(0, i);
683 |         numLevelsToRemove = 2;
684 |         i = name.indexOf('.', i + 11);
685 |         while (i >= 0) {
686 |           numLevelsToRemove++;
687 |           i = name.indexOf('.', i + 1);
688 |         }
689 |       }
690 | 
691 |       if (parent != null && NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
692 |         // Object literal keys have no prefix that's referenced directly per
693 |         // key, so we're done.
694 |         return true;
695 |       }
696 | 
697 |       for (int i = 0; i < numLevelsToRemove; i++) {
698 |         parent = n;
699 |         n = n.getFirstChild();
700 |       }
701 | 
702 |       handleGet(t, n, parent, prefix, Ref.Type.PROTOTYPE_GET);
703 |       return true;
704 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java

Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
776 |     if (item == null) {
777 |       return null;
778 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
779 |       return item.next;
780 |     } else {
781 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);
782 |       if (result != item.next) {
783 |         return new PropListItem(
784 |             item.type, item.intValue, item.objectValue, result);
785 |       } else {
786 |         return item;
787 |       }
788 |     }
789 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1107 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1108 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1109 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1110 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1111 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1112 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1113 |       } else {
1114 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1115 |       }
1116 |     }
1117 |     return builder.build();
1118 |   }

===== M0044 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.build()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 149-175
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a union.
   * @return A UnionType if it has two or more alternates, the
   *    only alternate if it has one and otherwise {@code NO_TYPE}.
   */
Method code:
149 |   JSType build() {
150 |     if (result == null) {
151 |       if (isAllType) {
152 |         result = registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
153 |       } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
154 |         if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
155 |           result = registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
156 |         } else {
157 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
158 |         }
159 |       } else {
160 |         int size = alternates.size();
161 |         if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
162 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
163 |         } else {
164 |           if (size > 1) {
165 |             result = new UnionType(registry, getAlternateListCopy());
166 |           } else if (size == 1) {
167 |             result = alternates.iterator().next();
168 |           } else {
169 |             result = registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
170 |           }
171 |         }
172 |       }
173 |     }
174 |     return result;
175 |   }

===== M0045 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 379-388
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
379 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
380 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
381 |          current != null;
382 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
383 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
384 |         return true;
385 |       }
386 |     }
387 |     return false;
388 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1520-1536
Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1520 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1521 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1522 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1523 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1524 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1525 |         next();
1526 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1527 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1528 |           next();
1529 |         }
1530 |         skipEOLs();
1531 |         token = next();
1532 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1533 |       }
1534 |     }
1535 |     return typeExpr;
1536 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java

Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0062 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 820-847
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
820 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
821 |     // unknown
822 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
823 |       return true;
824 |     }
825 |     // equality
826 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
827 |       return true;
828 |     }
829 |     // all type
830 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
831 |       return true;
832 |     }
833 |     // unions
834 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
835 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
836 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
837 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
838 |           return true;
839 |         }
840 |       }
841 |     }
842 |     // named types
843 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
844 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).referencedType);
845 |     }
846 |     return false;
847 |   }

185 |   @Override
186 |   public int hashCode() {
187 |     int hashCode = 0;
188 |     if (returnType != null) {
189 |       hashCode += returnType.hashCode();
190 |     }
191 |     if (returnTypeInferred) {
192 |       hashCode += 1;
193 |     }
194 |     if (parameters != null) {
195 |       Node param = parameters.getFirstChild();
196 |       while (param != null) {
197 |         JSType paramType = param.getJSType();
198 |         if (paramType != null) {

202 |       }
203 |     }
204 |     return hashCode;
205 |   }

===== M0065 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

565 |     @Override
566 |     Node processInfixExpression(InfixExpression exprNode) {
567 |       Node n =  newNode(
568 |           transformTokenType(exprNode.getType()),
569 |           transform(exprNode.getLeft()),
570 |           transform(exprNode.getRight()));
571 |       // Set the line number here so we can fine-tune it in ways transform
572 |       // doesn't do.

575 |       // line numbers from Node reference the operator token.  Add the offset
576 |       // to the operator to get the correct character number.

578 |           exprNode.getOperatorPosition()));

===== M0077 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.getValueType(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 407-429
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the type of a value or simple expression.
     *
     * @param n An rvalue in an assignment or variable declaration (not null)
     * @return A {@link Name.Type}
     */
Method code:
407 |     Name.Type getValueType(Node n) {
408 |       switch (n.getType()) {
409 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
410 |           return Name.Type.OBJECTLIT;
411 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
412 |           return Name.Type.FUNCTION;
413 |         case Token.OR:
414 |           // Recurse on the second value. If the first value were an object
415 |           // literal or function, then the OR would be meaningless and the
416 |           // second value would be dead code. Assume that if the second value
417 |           // is an object literal or function, then the first value will also
418 |           // evaluate to one when it doesn't evaluate to false.
419 |           return getValueType(n.getLastChild());
420 |         case Token.HOOK:
421 |           // The same line of reasoning used for the OR case applies here.
422 |           Node second = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
423 |           Name.Type t = getValueType(second);
424 |           if (t != Name.Type.OTHER) return t;
425 |           Node third = second.getNext();
426 |           return getValueType(third);
427 |       }
428 |       return Name.Type.OTHER;
429 |     }

===== M0085 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 215-251
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
215 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
216 |     this.options = options;
217 |     if (errorManager == null) {
218 |       if (outStream == null) {
219 |         setErrorManager(
220 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
221 |       } else {
222 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
223 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
224 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
225 |         setErrorManager(printer);
226 |       }
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
230 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
231 |     guards.add(
232 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
233 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
234 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
235 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
236 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
237 |     }
238 | 
239 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
240 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
241 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
242 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
243 |     // error off.
244 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
245 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
246 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
247 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
248 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
249 |     }
250 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
251 |   }

Original range: 118-281

Method code:
118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:
125 |       case Token.COMMA:
126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:
128 |       case Token.GE:
129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:

147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:

183 |       case Token.CALL:

185 |       case Token.CASE:

188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:

197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:

208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }
211 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
212 |       case Token.FALSE:
213 |       case Token.NULL:
214 |       case Token.THIS:
215 |       case Token.TRUE:
216 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:

238 |       case Token.LABEL:

240 |       case Token.LP:

242 |       case Token.NAME:

244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.VAR:

271 |                                           node.getClass() +
272 |                                           " type: " +
273 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
274 |         }
275 |       case Token.WHILE:
276 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
277 |       case Token.WITH:
278 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
279 |     }
280 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
281 |   }

   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
399 |   @Override
400 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
401 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
402 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
403 |     if (unknown) {
404 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
405 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
406 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
407 |         unknown = false;
408 |       } else {
409 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
410 |       }
411 |     }
412 |     return unknown;
413 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 250-293

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
250 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
251 |     this.type = type;
252 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
253 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
254 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
255 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
256 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
257 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
258 |       int i = 0;
259 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
260 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
261 |         i++;
262 |       }
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
266 | 
267 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
268 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
269 |       return;
270 |     }
271 | 
272 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
273 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
274 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
275 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
276 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
277 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
278 |       trace.truncateEvents();
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
282 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
283 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
284 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
285 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
286 |                     + "try/finally block. "
287 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
288 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
289 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     trace.startEvent(this);
293 |   }

/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect an
     * assignment to a global name in global scope.
     *
     * @param t The traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @param isPropAssign Whether this set corresponds to a property
     *     assignment of the form <code>a.b.c = ...;</code>
     * @param type The type of the value that the name is being assigned
     */
Method code:
443 |     void handleSetFromGlobal(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent, String name,
444 |                              boolean isPropAssign, Name.Type type) {
445 |       if (maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(t, n, parent, name)) return;
446 | 
447 |       Name nameObj = getOrCreateName(name);
448 |       nameObj.type = type;
449 | 
450 |       Ref set = new Ref(t, n, Ref.Type.SET_FROM_GLOBAL);
451 |       nameObj.addRef(set);
452 | 
453 |       if (isNestedAssign(parent)) {
454 |         // This assignment is both a set and a get that creates an alias.
455 |         Ref get = new Ref(t, n, Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET);
456 |         nameObj.addRef(get);
457 |         Ref.markTwins(set, get);
458 |       } else if (isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(n, parent)) {
459 |         // Names with a @constructor or @enum annotation are always collapsed
460 |         nameObj.setIsClassOrEnum();
461 |       }
462 |     }

===== M0130 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 204-228
Documentation:

Method code:
204 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
205 |     JSDocInfo jsDocInfo = handleJsDoc(node);
206 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
207 |     if (jsDocInfo != null) {
208 |       irNode.setJSDocInfo(jsDocInfo);
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
212 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
213 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
214 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
215 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
216 |     } else {
217 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
218 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
219 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
220 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
221 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
222 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
223 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
224 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
225 |       }
226 |     }
227 |     return irNode;
228 |   }

===== M0134 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 137-873
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
137 |   boolean parse() {
138 |     int lineno;
139 |     int charno;
140 | 
141 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
142 |     JSTypeExpression type;
143 | 
144 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
145 |     skipEOLs();
146 | 
147 |     JsDocToken token = next();
148 | 
149 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
150 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
151 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
152 |       token = blockInfo.token;
153 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
154 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
155 |       }
156 |     } else {
157 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
158 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
159 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
160 |         // what it was.
161 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
162 |       }
163 |     }
164 | 
165 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
166 |     retry: for (;;) {
167 |       switch (token) {
168 |         case ANNOTATION:
169 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
170 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
171 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
172 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
173 | 
174 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
175 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
176 |             if (annotation == null) {
177 |               parser.addWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
178 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
179 |             } else {
180 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
181 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
182 | 
183 |               switch (annotation) {
184 |                 case AUTHOR:
185 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
186 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
187 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
188 | 
189 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
190 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
191 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
192 |                     } else {
193 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
194 |                     }
195 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
196 |                   } else {
197 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
198 |                   }
199 |                   continue retry;
200 | 
201 |                 case CONSTANT:
202 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
203 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
204 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
205 |                   }
206 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
207 |                   continue retry;
208 | 
209 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
210 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
211 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
212 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
213 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
214 |                     } else {
215 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
216 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
217 |                     }
218 |                   }
219 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
220 |                   continue retry;
221 | 
222 |                 case DEPRECATED:
223 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
224 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
225 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |                   }
227 | 
228 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
229 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
230 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
231 | 
232 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
233 | 
234 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
235 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
236 |                   }
237 | 
238 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
239 |                   continue retry;
240 | 
241 |                 case INTERFACE:
242 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
243 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
244 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
245 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
246 |                     } else {
247 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
248 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
249 |                     }
250 |                   }
251 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
252 |                   continue retry;
253 | 
254 |                 case DESC:
255 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
256 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
257 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
258 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
259 |                     continue retry;
260 |                   } else {
261 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
262 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
263 | 
264 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
265 | 
266 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
267 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
268 |                     continue retry;
269 |                   }
270 | 
271 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
272 |                   String fileOverview = "";
273 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
274 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
275 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
276 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
277 | 
278 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
279 | 
280 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
281 |                   } else {
282 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
283 |                   }
284 | 
285 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview) ||
286 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
287 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
288 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
289 |                   }
290 |                   continue retry;
291 | 
292 |                 case LICENSE:
293 |                 case PRESERVE:
294 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
295 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
296 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
297 | 
298 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
299 | 
300 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
301 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
302 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
303 |                     }
304 |                   }
305 | 
306 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
307 |                   continue retry;
308 | 
309 |                 case ENUM:
310 |                   token = next();
311 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
312 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
313 | 
314 |                   type = null;
315 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
316 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
317 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
318 |                   }
319 | 
320 |                   if (type == null) {
321 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
322 |                   }
323 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
324 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
325 |                   }
326 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
327 |                   continue retry;
328 | 
329 |                 case EXPORT:
330 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
331 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
332 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
333 |                   }
334 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
335 |                   continue retry;
336 | 
337 |                 case EXTERNS:
338 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
339 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
340 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
341 |                   }
342 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
343 |                   continue retry;
344 | 
345 |                 case JAVA_DISPATCH:
346 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
347 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
348 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
349 |                   }
350 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
351 |                   continue retry;
352 | 
353 |                 case EXTENDS:
354 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
355 |                   skipEOLs();
356 |                   token = next();
357 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
358 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
359 |                   boolean matchingRc = false;
360 | 
361 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
362 |                     token = next();
363 |                     matchingRc = true;
364 |                   }
365 | 
366 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
367 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
368 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
369 | 
370 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
371 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
372 | 
373 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
374 |                     if (typeNode != null && !matchingRc) {
375 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
376 |                     }
377 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
378 | 
379 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
380 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordBaseType(type)) {
381 |                         parser.addWarning(
382 |                             "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
383 |                       }
384 |                     } else {
385 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
386 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
387 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
388 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
389 |                             lineno, charno);
390 |                       }
391 |                     }
392 |                     token = next();
393 |                     if (matchingRc) {
394 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
395 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
396 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
397 |                       }
398 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
399 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
400 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
401 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
402 |                     }
403 |                   } else {
404 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
405 |                   }
406 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
407 |                   continue retry;
408 | 
409 |                 case HIDDEN:
410 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
411 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
412 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
413 |                   }
414 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
415 |                   continue retry;
416 | 
417 |                 case LENDS:
418 |                   skipEOLs();
419 | 
420 |                   matchingRc = false;
421 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.LC)) {
422 |                     token = next();
423 |                     matchingRc = true;
424 |                   }
425 | 
426 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
427 |                     token = next();
428 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordLends(stream.getString())) {
429 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.incompatible",
430 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
431 |                     }
432 |                   } else {
433 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.missing",
434 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
435 |                   }
436 | 
437 |                   if (matchingRc && !match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
438 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
439 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
440 |                   }
441 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
442 |                   continue retry;
443 | 
444 |                 case MEANING:
445 |                   ExtractionInfo meaningInfo =
446 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
447 |                   String meaning = meaningInfo.string;
448 |                   token = meaningInfo.token;
449 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordMeaning(meaning)) {
450 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.meaning.extra",
451 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
452 |                   }
453 |                   continue retry;
454 | 
455 |                 case NO_ALIAS:
456 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoAlias()) {
457 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.noalias",
458 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
459 |                   }
460 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
461 |                   continue retry;
462 | 
463 |                 case NO_COMPILE:
464 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoCompile()) {
465 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocompile",
466 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
467 |                   }
468 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
469 |                   continue retry;
470 | 
471 |                 case NO_TYPE_CHECK:
472 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
473 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
474 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
475 |                   }
476 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
477 |                   continue retry;
478 | 
479 |                 case NOT_IMPLEMENTED:
480 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
481 |                   continue retry;
482 | 
483 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
484 |                 case OVERRIDE:
485 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {
486 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.override",
487 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
488 |                   }
489 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
490 |                   continue retry;
491 | 
492 |                 case THROWS:
493 |                   skipEOLs();
494 |                   token = next();
495 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
496 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
497 |                   type = null;
498 | 
499 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
500 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
501 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
502 | 
503 |                     if (type == null) {
504 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
505 |                       // recovering parsing
506 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
507 |                       continue retry;
508 |                     }
509 |                   }
510 | 
511 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
512 |                   token = current();
513 | 
514 |                   // Save the throw type.
515 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);
516 | 
517 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).
518 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
519 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
520 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
521 | 
522 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
523 | 
524 |                     if (description.length() > 0) {
525 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
526 |                     }
527 | 
528 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
529 |                   } else {
530 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
531 |                   }
532 |                   continue retry;
533 | 
534 |                 case PARAM:
535 |                   skipEOLs();
536 |                   token = next();
537 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
538 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
539 |                   type = null;
540 | 
541 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
542 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
543 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
544 | 
545 |                     if (type == null) {
546 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
547 |                       // recovering parsing
548 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
549 |                       continue retry;
550 |                     }
551 |                     skipEOLs();
552 |                     token = next();
553 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
554 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
555 |                   }
556 | 
557 |                   String name = null;
558 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
559 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
560 |                     token = next();
561 |                   }
562 | 
563 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
564 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
565 |                         lineno, charno);
566 |                   } else {
567 |                     name = stream.getString();
568 | 
569 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
570 |                       token = next();
571 | 
572 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter annotation.
573 |                       // It makes no sense under our type system.
574 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
575 |                         token = next();
576 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
577 |                           token = next();
578 |                         }
579 |                       }
580 | 
581 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
582 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
583 |                       } else if (type != null) {
584 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
585 |                         // already.
586 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
587 |                       }
588 |                     }
589 | 
590 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
591 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
592 |                     // for handling properties of params.
593 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
594 |                       name = null;
595 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
596 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
597 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
598 |                             lineno, charno);
599 |                       } else {
600 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
601 |                             lineno, charno);
602 |                       }
603 |                     }
604 |                   }
605 | 
606 |                   if (name == null) {
607 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
608 |                     continue retry;
609 |                   }
610 | 
611 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, lineno, charno);
612 | 
613 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
614 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
615 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
616 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
617 | 
618 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
619 | 
620 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
621 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
622 |                           paramDescription);
623 |                     }
624 | 
625 |                     token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
626 |                   } else {
627 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
628 |                   }
629 |                   continue retry;
630 | 
631 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
632 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
633 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
634 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
635 |                   }
636 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
637 |                   continue retry;
638 | 
639 |                 case PRIVATE:
640 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
641 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
642 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
643 |                   }
644 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
645 |                   continue retry;
646 | 
647 |                 case PROTECTED:
648 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
649 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
650 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
651 |                   }
652 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
653 |                   continue retry;
654 | 
655 |                 case PUBLIC:
656 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
657 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
658 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
659 |                   }
660 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
661 |                   continue retry;
662 | 
663 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
664 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
665 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
666 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
667 |                   }
668 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
669 |                   continue retry;
670 | 
671 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
672 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
673 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
674 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
675 |                   }
676 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
677 |                   continue retry;
678 | 
679 |                 case MODIFIES:
680 |                   token = parseModifiesTag(next());
681 |                   continue retry;
682 | 
683 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
684 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
685 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
686 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
687 |                   }
688 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
689 |                   continue retry;
690 | 
691 |                 case SEE:
692 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
693 |                     ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
694 |                     String reference = referenceInfo.string;
695 | 
696 |                     if (reference.length() == 0) {
697 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
698 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
699 |                     } else {
700 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
701 |                     }
702 | 
703 |                     token = referenceInfo.token;
704 |                   } else {
705 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
706 |                   }
707 |                   continue retry;
708 | 
709 |                 case SUPPRESS:
710 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
711 |                   continue retry;
712 | 
713 |                 case TEMPLATE:
714 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
715 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
716 | 
717 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
718 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
719 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
720 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
721 |                       templateTypeName)) {
722 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
723 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
724 |                   }
725 | 
726 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
727 |                   continue retry;
728 | 
729 |                 case VERSION:
730 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
731 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
732 | 
733 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
734 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
735 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
736 |                   } else {
737 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
738 |                        parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
739 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
740 |                     }
741 |                   }
742 | 
743 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
744 |                   continue retry;
745 | 
746 |                 case DEFINE:
747 |                 case RETURN:
748 |                 case THIS:
749 |                 case TYPE:
750 |                 case TYPEDEF:
751 |                   skipEOLs();
752 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
753 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
754 | 
755 |                   token = next();
756 | 
757 |                   Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token, lineno, charno);
758 | 
759 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
760 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
761 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
762 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
763 |                     }
764 |                   }
765 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
766 | 
767 |                   if (type == null) {
768 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
769 |                     // recovering parsing
770 |                   } else {
771 |                     switch (annotation) {
772 |                       case DEFINE:
773 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
774 |                           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
775 |                               lineno, charno);
776 |                         }
777 |                         break;
778 | 
779 |                       case RETURN:
780 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
781 |                           parser.addWarning(
782 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
783 |                           break;
784 |                         }
785 | 
786 |                         // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
787 |                         token = current();
788 | 
789 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
790 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
791 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
792 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
793 | 
794 |                           String returnDescription =
795 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
796 | 
797 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
798 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
799 |                                 returnDescription);
800 |                           }
801 | 
802 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
803 |                         } else {
804 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
805 |                         }
806 |                         continue retry;
807 | 
808 |                       case THIS:
809 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
810 |                           parser.addWarning(
811 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
812 |                         }
813 |                         break;
814 | 
815 |                       case TYPE:
816 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
817 |                           parser.addWarning(
818 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
819 |                         }
820 |                         break;
821 | 
822 |                       case TYPEDEF:
823 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
824 |                           parser.addWarning(
825 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
826 |                         }
827 |                         break;
828 |                     }
829 | 
830 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
831 |                   }
832 |                   continue retry;
833 |               }
834 |             }
835 |           }
836 |           break;
837 | 
838 |         case EOC:
839 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
840 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
841 |           }
842 | 
843 |           return true;
844 | 
845 |         case EOF:
846 |           // discard any accumulated information
847 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
848 |           parser.addWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
849 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
850 |           return false;
851 | 
852 |         case EOL:
853 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
854 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
855 |           }
856 |           token = next();
857 |           continue retry;
858 | 
859 |         default:
860 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
861 |             token = next();
862 |             continue retry;
863 |           } else {
864 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
865 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
866 |             continue retry;
867 |           }
868 |       }
869 | 
870 |       // next token
871 |       token = next();
872 |     }
873 |   }

/**
   * Finds all defines, and creates a {@link DefineInfo} data structure for
   * each one.
   * @return A map of {@link DefineInfo} structures, keyed by name.
   */
Method code:
171 |   private Map<String, DefineInfo> collectDefines(Node root,
172 |       GlobalNamespace namespace) {
173 |     // Find all the global names with a @define annotation
174 |     List<Name> allDefines = Lists.newArrayList();
175 |     for (Name name : namespace.getNameIndex().values()) {
176 |       if (name.docInfo != null && name.docInfo.isDefine()) {
177 |         // Process defines should not depend on check types being enabled,
178 |         // so we look for the JSDoc instead of the inferred type.
179 |         if (isValidDefineType(name.docInfo.getType())) {
180 |           allDefines.add(name);
181 |         } else {
182 |           JSError error = JSError.make(
183 |               name.declaration.sourceName,
184 |               name.declaration.node,
185 |               INVALID_DEFINE_TYPE_ERROR);
186 |           compiler.report(error);
187 |         }
188 |       } else if (name.refs != null) {
189 |         for (Ref ref : name.refs) {
190 |           Node n = ref.node;
191 |           Node parent = ref.node.getParent();
192 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
193 |           if (info == null &&
194 |               parent.getType() == Token.VAR && parent.hasOneChild()) {
195 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
196 |           }
197 | 
198 |           if (info != null && info.isDefine()) {
199 |             allDefines.add(name);
200 |             break;
201 |           }
202 |         }
203 |       }
204 |     }
205 | 
206 |     CollectDefines pass = new CollectDefines(compiler, allDefines);
207 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, pass);
208 |     return pass.getAllDefines();
209 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1326-1475
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope, boolean) */
Method code:
1326 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1327 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope, boolean forgiving) {
1328 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1329 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1330 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1331 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1332 | 
1333 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1334 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1335 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, forgiving)
1336 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1337 | 
1338 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1339 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1340 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1341 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1342 |         }
1343 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1344 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1345 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope, forgiving));
1346 | 
1347 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1348 |         return createOptionalType(
1349 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1350 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1351 | 
1352 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1353 |         return createOptionalType(
1354 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1355 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1356 | 
1357 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1358 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1359 | 
1360 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1361 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1362 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1363 | 
1364 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1365 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1366 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1367 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1368 |           builder.addAlternate(
1369 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope, false));
1370 |         }
1371 |         return builder.build();
1372 | 
1373 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1374 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1375 | 
1376 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1377 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1378 | 
1379 |       case Token.STRING:
1380 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1381 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1382 |         if (forgiving) {
1383 |           namedType.forgiveUnknownNames();
1384 |         }
1385 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1386 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1387 |         }
1388 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1389 |             !(enumTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1390 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1391 |           if (typeList != null &&
1392 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1393 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1394 |             JSType parameterType =
1395 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1396 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope, false);
1397 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1398 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1399 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1400 |               JSType indexType =
1401 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1402 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false);
1403 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1404 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1405 |             }
1406 |           }
1407 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1408 |         } else {
1409 |           return namedType;
1410 |         }
1411 | 
1412 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1413 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1414 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1415 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS) {
1416 |           Node thisNode = current.getFirstChild();
1417 |           thisType =
1418 |               ObjectType.cast(
1419 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1420 |                       thisNode, sourceName, scope, false)
1421 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1422 |           if (thisType == null) {
1423 |             reporter.warning(
1424 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject"),
1425 |                 sourceName, thisNode.getLineno(), "", thisNode.getCharno());
1426 |           }
1427 | 
1428 |           current = current.getNext();
1429 |         }
1430 | 
1431 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1432 | 
1433 |         if (current.getType() == Token.LP) {
1434 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1435 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1436 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1437 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1438 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1439 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1440 |               } else {
1441 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1442 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1443 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1444 |               }
1445 |             } else {
1446 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1447 |                   arg, sourceName, scope, false);
1448 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1449 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1450 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1451 |                   reporter.warning(
1452 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1453 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), "", arg.getCharno());
1454 |                 }
1455 |               } else {
1456 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1457 |               }
1458 |             }
1459 |           }
1460 |           current = current.getNext();
1461 |         }
1462 | 
1463 |         JSType returnType =
1464 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope, false);
1465 | 
1466 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1467 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1468 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1469 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1470 |             .build();
1471 |     }
1472 | 
1473 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1474 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1475 |   }

===== M0152 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1146-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

===== M0159 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 361-376
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
361 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
362 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
363 |       Set<String> directives = null;
364 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
365 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
366 |         if (directives == null) {
367 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
368 |         } else {
369 |           directives.add(directive);
370 |         }
371 |       }
372 | 
373 |       if (directives != null) {
374 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
375 |       }
376 |     }

===== M0163 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

Documentation:
/**
     * Tracks the given define.
     *
     * @param t The current traversal, for context.
     * @param name The full name for this define.
     * @param value The value assigned to the define.
     * @param valueParent The parent node of value.
     * @return Whether we should remove this assignment from the parse tree.
     */
Method code:
403 |     private boolean processDefineAssignment(NodeTraversal t,
404 |         String name, Node value, Node valueParent) {
405 |       if (value == null || !NodeUtil.isValidDefineValue(value,
406 |                                                         allDefines.keySet())) {
407 |         compiler.report(
408 |             t.makeError(value, INVALID_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, name));
409 |       } else if (!isAssignAllowed()) {
410 |         compiler.report(
411 |             t.makeError(valueParent, NON_GLOBAL_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, name));
412 |       } else {
413 |         DefineInfo info = allDefines.get(name);
414 |         if (info == null) {
415 |           // First declaration of this define.
416 |           info = new DefineInfo(value, valueParent);
417 |           allDefines.put(name, info);
418 |           assignableDefines.put(name, info);
419 |         } else if (info.recordAssignment(value)) {
420 |           // The define was already initialized, but this is a safe
421 |           // re-assignment.
422 |           return true;
423 |         } else {
424 |           // The define was already initialized, and this is an unsafe
425 |           // re-assignment.
426 |           compiler.report(
427 |               t.makeError(valueParent, DEFINE_NOT_ASSIGNABLE_ERROR,
428 |                   name, info.getReasonWhyNotAssignable()));
429 |         }
430 |       }
431 | 
432 |       return false;
433 |     }

254 |     // can not unread past across line boundary
255 |     assert(!(ungetCursor != 0 && ungetBuffer[ungetCursor - 1] == '\n'));

===== M0166 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isValidDefineValue(Node,Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 320-347
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given value may be assigned to a define.
   *
   * @param val The value being assigned.
   * @param defines The list of names of existing defines.
   */
Method code:
320 |   static boolean isValidDefineValue(Node val, Set<String> defines) {
321 |     switch (val.getType()) {
322 |       case Token.STRING:
323 |       case Token.NUMBER:
324 |       case Token.TRUE:
325 |       case Token.FALSE:
326 |         return true;
327 | 
328 |       // Binary operators are only valid if both children are valid.
329 |       case Token.BITAND:
330 |       case Token.BITNOT:
331 |       case Token.BITOR:
332 |       case Token.BITXOR:
333 | 
334 |       // Uniary operators are valid if the child is valid.
335 |       case Token.NOT:
336 |       case Token.NEG:
337 |         return isValidDefineValue(val.getFirstChild(), defines);
338 | 
339 |       // Names are valid if and only if they are defines themselves.
340 |       case Token.NAME:
341 |       case Token.GETPROP:
342 |         if (val.isQualifiedName()) {
343 |           return defines.contains(val.getQualifiedName());
344 |         }
345 |     }
346 |     return false;
347 |   }
