===== M0168 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 230-323
Documentation:

Method code:
230 |     @Override
231 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
232 |       if (nodeFilter != null && !nodeFilter.apply(n)) {
233 |         return;
234 |       }
235 | 
236 |       // If we are traversing the externs, then we save a pointer to the scope
237 |       // generated by them, so that we can do lookups in it later.
238 |       if (externsRoot != null && n == externsRoot) {
239 |         externsScope = t.getScope();
240 |       }
241 | 
242 |       String name;
243 |       boolean isSet = false;
244 |       Name.Type type = Name.Type.OTHER;
245 |       boolean isPropAssign = false;
246 | 
247 |       switch (n.getType()) {
248 |         case Token.STRING:
249 |           // This may be a key in an object literal declaration.
250 |           name = null;
251 |           if (parent != null && parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
252 |             name = getNameForObjLitKey(n);
253 |           }
254 |           if (name == null) return;
255 |           isSet = true;
256 |           type = getValueType(n.getNext());
257 |           break;
258 |         case Token.NAME:
259 |           // This may be a variable get or set.
260 |           if (parent != null) {
261 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
262 |               case Token.VAR:
263 |                 isSet = true;
264 |                 Node rvalue = n.getFirstChild();
265 |                 type = rvalue == null ? Name.Type.OTHER : getValueType(rvalue);
266 |                 break;
267 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
268 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
269 |                   isSet = true;
270 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
271 |                 }
272 |                 break;
273 |               case Token.GETPROP:
274 |                 return;
275 |               case Token.FUNCTION:
276 |                 Node gramps = parent.getParent();
277 |                 if (gramps == null ||
278 |                     NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) return;
279 |                 isSet = true;
280 |                 type = Name.Type.FUNCTION;
281 |                 break;
282 |             }
283 |           }
284 |           name = n.getString();
285 |           break;
286 |         case Token.GETPROP:
287 |           // This may be a namespaced name get or set.
288 |           if (parent != null) {
289 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
290 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
291 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
292 |                   isSet = true;
293 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
294 |                   isPropAssign = true;
295 |                 }
296 |                 break;
297 |               case Token.GETPROP:
298 |                 return;
299 |             }
300 |           }
301 |           name = n.getQualifiedName();
302 |           if (name == null) return;
303 |           break;
304 |         default:
305 |           return;
306 |       }
307 | 
308 |       // We are only interested in global names.
309 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
310 |       if (!isGlobalNameReference(name, scope)) {
311 |         return;
312 |       }
313 | 
314 |       if (isSet) {
315 |         if (isGlobalScope(scope)) {
316 |           handleSetFromGlobal(t, n, parent, name, isPropAssign, type);
317 |         } else {
318 |           handleSetFromLocal(t, n, parent, name);
319 |         }
320 |       } else {
321 |         handleGet(t, n, parent, name);
322 |       }
323 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1865-1879
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1865 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1866 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1867 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1868 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1869 |       n2clone.parent = result;
1870 |       if (result.last != null) {
1871 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
1872 |       }
1873 |       if (result.first == null) {
1874 |         result.first = n2clone;
1875 |       }
1876 |       result.last = n2clone;
1877 |     }
1878 |     return result;
1879 |   }

===== M0187 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.CollectDefines.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 275-359
Documentation:

Method code:
275 |     public  void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
276 |       RefInfo refInfo = allRefInfo.get(n);
277 |       if (refInfo != null) {
278 |         Ref ref = refInfo.ref;
279 |         Name name = refInfo.name;
280 |         String fullName = name.fullName();
281 |         switch (ref.type) {
282 |           case SET_FROM_GLOBAL:
283 |           case SET_FROM_LOCAL:
284 |             Node valParent = getValueParent(ref);
285 |             Node val = valParent.getLastChild();
286 |             if (valParent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN && name.isSimpleName() &&
287 |                 name.declaration == ref) {
288 |               // For defines, it's an error if a simple name is assigned
289 |               // before it's declared
290 |               compiler.report(
291 |                   t.makeError(val, INVALID_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, fullName));
292 |             } else if (processDefineAssignment(t, fullName, val, valParent)) {
293 |               // remove the assignment so that the variable is still declared,
294 |               // but no longer assigned to a value, e.g.,
295 |               // DEF_FOO = 5; // becomes "5;"
296 | 
297 |               // We can't remove the ASSIGN/VAR when we're still visiting its
298 |               // children, so we'll have to come back later to remove it.
299 |               refInfo.name.removeRef(ref);
300 |               lvalueToRemoveLater = valParent;
301 |             }
302 |             break;
303 |           default:
304 |             if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
305 |               // Treat this as a reference to a define in the global scope.
306 |               // After this point, the define must not be reassigned,
307 |               // or it's an error.
308 |               DefineInfo info = assignableDefines.get(fullName);
309 |               if (info != null) {
310 |                 setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
311 |                 assignableDefines.remove(fullName);
312 |               }
313 |             }
314 |             break;
315 |         }
316 |       }
317 | 
318 |       if (!t.inGlobalScope() &&
319 |           n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isDefine()) {
320 |         // warn about @define annotations in local scopes
321 |         compiler.report(
322 |             t.makeError(n, NON_GLOBAL_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, ""));
323 |       }
324 | 
325 |       if (lvalueToRemoveLater == n) {
326 |         lvalueToRemoveLater = null;
327 |         if (n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
328 |           Node last = n.getLastChild();
329 |           n.removeChild(last);
330 |           parent.replaceChild(n, last);
331 |         } else {
332 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.NAME);
333 |           n.removeChild(n.getFirstChild());
334 |         }
335 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
336 |       }
337 | 
338 |       if (n.getType() == Token.CALL) {
339 |         if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
340 |           // If there's a function call in the global scope,
341 |           // we just say it's unsafe and freeze all the defines.
342 |           //
343 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): We could be a lot smarter here. For example,
344 |           // ReplaceOverriddenVars keeps a call graph of all functions and
345 |           // which functions/variables that they reference, and tries
346 |           // to statically determine which functions are "safe" and which
347 |           // are not. But this would be overkill, expecially because
348 |           // the intended use of defines is with config_files, where
349 |           // all the defines are at the top of the bundle.
350 |           for (DefineInfo info : assignableDefines.values()) {
351 |             setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
352 |           }
353 | 
354 |           assignableDefines.clear();
355 |         }
356 |       }
357 | 
358 |       updateAssignAllowedStack(n, false);
359 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0196 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 310-317
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
311 |     if (c <= 127) {
312 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0
315 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
316 |     }
317 |   }

/**
     * Gets a {@link Name} instance for a global name. Creates it if necessary,
     * as well as instances for any of its prefixes that are not yet defined.
     *
     * @param name A global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c.d")
     * @return The {@link Name} instance for {@code name}
     */
Method code:
726 |     Name getOrCreateName(String name) {
727 |       Name node = nameMap.get(name);
728 |       if (node == null) {
729 |         int i = name.lastIndexOf('.');
730 |         if (i >= 0) {
731 |           String parentName = name.substring(0, i);
732 |           Name parent = getOrCreateName(parentName);
733 |           node = parent.addProperty(name.substring(i + 1), inExterns);
734 |         } else {
735 |           node = new Name(name, null, inExterns);
736 |           globalNames.add(node);
737 |         }
738 |         nameMap.put(name, node);
739 |       }
740 |       return node;
741 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1496-1509
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a node represents an object literal key
   * (e.g. key1 in {key1: value1, key2: value2}).
   *
   * @param node A node
   * @param parent The node's parent
   */
Method code:
1496 |   static boolean isObjectLitKey(Node node, Node parent) {
1497 |     if (node.getType() == Token.STRING && parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
1498 |       int index = 0;
1499 |       for (Node current = parent.getFirstChild();
1500 |            current != null;
1501 |            current = current.getNext()) {
1502 |         if (current == node) {
1503 |           return index % 2 == 0;
1504 |         }
1505 |         index++;
1506 |       }
1507 |     }
1508 |     return false;
1509 |   }

   */

394 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
395 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
396 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();

89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),

91 |           logger_);

===== M0251 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1726-1740
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1726 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1727 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1728 |       return getString();
1729 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1730 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1731 |       if (left == null) {
1732 |         return null;
1733 |       }
1734 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1735 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1736 |       return "this";
1737 |     } else {
1738 |       return null;
1739 |     }
1740 |   }

===== M0256 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 83-113
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
84 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
85 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
86 | 
87 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
88 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
89 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
90 | 
91 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
92 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
93 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
94 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
95 |         declareVar(fnName, fnNameNode, n, null, null, n);
96 |       }
97 | 
98 |       // Args: Declare function variables
99 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
100 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
101 |            a = a.getNext()) {
102 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
103 |         declareVar(a.getString(), a, args, n, null, n);
104 |       }
105 | 
106 |       // Body
107 |       scanVars(body, n);
108 |     } else {
109 |       // It's the global block
110 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
111 |       scanVars(n, null);
112 |     }
113 |   }

===== M0270 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 406-450
Documentation:

Method code:
406 |   @Override
407 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
408 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
409 |       return true;
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     // Union types
413 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
414 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
415 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
416 |       return false;
417 |     }
418 | 
419 |     // record types
420 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
421 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
422 |     }
423 | 
424 |     // Interfaces
425 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
426 |     // to the interface instance.
427 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
428 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
429 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
430 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
431 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
432 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
433 |           return true;
434 |         }
435 |       }
436 |     }
437 | 
438 |     // other prototype based objects
439 |     if (that != null) {
440 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
441 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
442 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
443 |         // to avoid guessing.
444 |         return true;
445 |       }
446 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     return false;
450 |   }

===== M0314 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 49-86
Documentation:

Method code:
49 |   @Override
50 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
51 |     Node node = error.node;
52 |     if (node != null) {
53 |       for (Node current = node;
54 |            current != null;
55 |            current = current.getParent()) {
56 |         int type = current.getType();
57 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
58 | 
59 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
60 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
61 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
62 |         // a bad idea.
63 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
64 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(current);
65 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
66 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
67 |         }
68 | 
69 |         if (info != null) {
70 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
71 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
72 | 
73 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
74 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
75 |             if (guard != null) {
76 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
77 |               if (newLevel != null) {
78 |                 return newLevel;
79 |               }
80 |             }
81 |           }
82 |         }
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 |     return null;
86 |   }

===== M0315 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(CodingConvention,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2169-2180
Documentation:
/** Whether the given name is constant by coding convention. */
Method code:
2169 |   static boolean isConstantByConvention(
2170 |       CodingConvention convention, Node node, Node parent) {
2171 |     String name = node.getString();
2172 |     if (parent.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
2173 |         node == parent.getLastChild()) {
2174 |       return convention.isConstantKey(name);
2175 |     } else if (isObjectLitKey(node, parent)) {
2176 |       return convention.isConstantKey(name);
2177 |     } else {
2178 |       return convention.isConstant(name);
2179 |     }
2180 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Skips all EOLs and all empty lines in the JSDoc. Call this method if you
   * want the JSDoc entry to span multiple lines.
   */
Method code:
2146 |   private void skipEOLs() {
2147 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL)) {
2148 |       next();
2149 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STAR)) {
2150 |         next();
2151 |       }
2152 |     }
2153 |   }

===== M0333 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1646-1668
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1646 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1647 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1648 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1649 |     }
1650 | 
1651 |     Node typeName = newStringNode(stream.getString());
1652 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1653 |       next();
1654 |       skipEOLs();
1655 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1656 |       if (memberType != null) {
1657 |         typeName.addChildToFront(memberType);
1658 | 
1659 |         skipEOLs();
1660 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1661 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1662 |         }
1663 | 
1664 |         next();
1665 |       }
1666 |     }
1667 |     return typeName;
1668 |   }

===== M0353 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 120-170
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
121 |       // null is the smallest value
122 |       if (p2 == null) {
123 |         if (p1 == null) {
124 |           return 0;
125 |         } else {
126 |           return P1_GT_P2;
127 |         }
128 |       }
129 | 
130 |       // check level
131 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
132 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
133 |       }
134 | 
135 |       // sourceName comparison
136 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
137 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
138 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
139 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
140 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
141 |           return sourceCompare;
142 |         }
143 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
144 |         return P1_LT_P2;
145 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
146 |         return P1_GT_P2;
147 |       }
148 |       // lineno comparison
149 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
150 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
151 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
152 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
153 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
154 |         return P1_LT_P2;
155 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
156 |         return P1_GT_P2;
157 |       }
158 |       // charno comparison
159 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
160 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
161 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
162 |         return charno1 - charno2;
163 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
164 |         return P1_LT_P2;
165 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
166 |         return P1_GT_P2;
167 |       }
168 |       // description
169 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
170 |     }

===== M0356 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 777-791
Documentation:

Method code:
777 |     @Override
778 |     Node processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression exprNode) {
779 |       int type = transformTokenType(exprNode.getType());
780 |       Node operand = transform(exprNode.getOperand());
781 |       if (type == Token.NEG && operand.getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
782 |         operand.setDouble(-operand.getDouble());
783 |         return operand;
784 |       } else {
785 |         Node node = newNode(type, operand);
786 |         if (exprNode.isPostfix()) {
787 |           node.putBooleanProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP, true);
788 |         }
789 |         return node;
790 |       }
791 |     }

===== M0357 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1569-1608
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1569 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1570 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1571 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1572 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1573 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1574 |       // {?} - right curly
1575 |       // {?=} - equals
1576 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1577 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1578 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1579 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1580 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1581 |       token = next();
1582 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1583 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1584 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1585 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1586 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1587 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1588 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1589 |       }
1590 | 
1591 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1592 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1593 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1594 |     } else {
1595 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1596 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1597 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1598 |           next();
1599 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1600 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1601 |           next();
1602 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1603 |         }
1604 |       }
1605 | 
1606 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1607 |     }

===== M0372 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 474-497
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a set operation is a constructor or enumeration
     * declaration. The set operation may either be an assignment to a name,
     * a variable declaration, or an object literal key mapping.
     *
     * @param n The node that represents the name being set
     * @param parent Parent node of {@code n} (an ASSIGN, VAR, or OBJLIT node)
     * @return Whether the set operation is either a constructor or enum
     *     declaration
     */
Method code:
474 |     private boolean isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node n, Node parent) {
475 |       JSDocInfo info;
476 |       int valueNodeType;
477 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
478 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
479 |           info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
480 |           valueNodeType = n.getNext().getType();
481 |           break;
482 |         case Token.VAR:
483 |           info = n.getJSDocInfo();
484 |           if (info == null) {
485 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
486 |           }
487 |           Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
488 |           valueNodeType = valueNode != null ? valueNode.getType() : Token.VOID;
489 |           break;
490 |         default:
491 |           return false;
492 |       }
493 |       // Heed the annotations only if they're sensibly used.
494 |       return info != null &&
495 |              (info.isConstructor() && valueNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
496 |               info.hasEnumParameterType() && valueNodeType == Token.OBJECTLIT);
497 |     }

   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named

   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
587 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
588 |     ObjectType owner = null;
589 | 
590 |     // Properties can only be defined on object types, so normalize everything
591 |     // to discrete object types.
592 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType) {
593 |       owner = (ObjectType) type;
594 |     } else if (getNativeType(ALL_TYPE).isSubtype(type)) {
595 |       owner = getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE);
596 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
597 |       for (JSType alternate : ((UnionType) type).getAlternates()) {
598 |         registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, alternate);
599 |       }
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     if (owner == null) {
603 |       return;
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
607 |     if (typeSet == null) {
608 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(
609 |           propertyName, typeSet = new LinkedHashSet<ObjectType>());
610 |     }
611 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
612 |     typeSet.add(owner);
613 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 420-446
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
420 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
421 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
422 | 
423 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
424 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
425 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
426 |       return 0;
427 |     }
428 | 
429 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
430 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
431 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
432 |       // a new statistic may have been added
433 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
434 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
435 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
436 |       }
437 |     }
438 | 
439 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
440 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
441 |       return 0;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
445 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
446 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Verifies that at least one module has been provided and that the first one
   * has at least one source code input.
   */
Method code:
358 |   private void checkFirstModule(List<JSModule> modules) {
359 |     if (modules.isEmpty()) {
360 |       report(JSError.make(EMPTY_MODULE_LIST_ERROR));
361 |     } else if (modules.get(0).getInputs().isEmpty() && modules.size() > 1) {
362 |       // The root module may only be empty if there is exactly 1 module.
363 |       report(JSError.make(EMPTY_ROOT_MODULE_ERROR,
364 |           modules.get(0).getName()));

===== M0432 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 118-174
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
118 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
119 |     switch (n.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.VAR:
121 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
122 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
123 |              child != null;) {
124 |           Node next = child.getNext();
125 |           Preconditions.checkState(child.getType() == Token.NAME);
126 | 
127 |           String name = child.getString();
128 |           declareVar(name, child, n, parent, null, n);
129 |           child = next;
130 |         }
131 |         return;
132 | 
133 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
134 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
135 |           return;
136 |         }
137 | 
138 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
139 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
140 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
141 |           return;
142 |         }
143 |         declareVar(fnName, n.getFirstChild(), n, parent, null, n);
144 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
145 | 
146 |       case Token.CATCH:
147 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
148 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
149 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
150 |         // is the code block
151 | 
152 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
153 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
154 | 
155 |         declareVar(var.getString(), var, n, parent, null, n);
156 |         scanVars(block, n);
157 |         return;  // only one child to scan
158 | 
159 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
160 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
161 |         break;
162 |     }
163 | 
164 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
165 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
166 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
167 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
168 |            child != null;) {
169 |         Node next = child.getNext();
170 |         scanVars(child, n);
171 |         child = next;
172 |       }
173 |     }
174 |   }

Method code:
271 |   private static boolean isAlpha(int c) {
272 |     // Use 'Z' < 'a'
273 |     if (c <= 'Z') {
274 |       return 'A' <= c;
275 |     } else {
276 |       return 'a' <= c && c <= 'z';
277 |     }
278 |   }

===== M0444 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 555-569
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
555 |   public Scope getScope() {
556 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
557 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
558 |       return scope;
559 |     }
560 | 
561 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
562 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
563 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
564 |       scopes.push(scope);
565 |     }
566 |     scopeRoots.clear();
567 | 
568 |     return scope;
569 |   }

===== M0453 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 60-77
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
60 |   @Override
61 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
62 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
63 |       return false;
64 |     }
65 | 
66 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
67 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
68 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
69 |     if (pos >= 0) {
70 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
71 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
72 |         return false;
73 |       }
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     return isConstantKey(name);
77 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 80-114
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
80 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
81 |                            String sourceString,
82 |                            Config config,
83 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
84 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
85 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
86 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
87 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
88 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
89 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
90 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
91 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
92 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
93 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
94 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
95 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
99 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
100 |     try {
101 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
102 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
103 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
104 |     } finally {
105 |       Context.exit();
106 |     }
107 |     Node root = null;
108 |     if (astRoot != null) {
109 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
110 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
111 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
112 |     }
113 |     return root;
114 |   }

===== M0467 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1614-1639
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1614 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1615 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1616 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1617 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1618 |       skipEOLs();
1619 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1620 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1621 |       skipEOLs();
1622 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1623 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1624 |       skipEOLs();
1625 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1626 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1627 |       String string = stream.getString();
1628 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1629 |         skipEOLs();
1630 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1631 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1632 |         return newStringNode(string);
1633 |       } else {
1634 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1635 |       }
1636 |     }
1637 | 
1638 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1639 |   }

===== M0473 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.overrideDefines(Map<String, DefineInfo>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 122-150
Documentation:

Method code:
122 |   private void overrideDefines(Map<String, DefineInfo> allDefines) {
123 |     boolean changed = false;
124 |     for (Map.Entry<String, DefineInfo> def : allDefines.entrySet()) {
125 |       String defineName = def.getKey();
126 |       DefineInfo info = def.getValue();
127 |       Node inputValue = dominantReplacements.get(defineName);
128 |       Node finalValue = inputValue != null ?
129 |           inputValue : info.getLastValue();
130 |       if (finalValue != info.initialValue) {
131 |         info.initialValueParent.replaceChild(
132 |             info.initialValue, finalValue.cloneTree());
133 |         compiler.addToDebugLog("Overriding @define variable " + defineName);
134 |         changed = changed ||
135 |             finalValue.getType() != info.initialValue.getType() ||
136 |             !finalValue.isEquivalentTo(info.initialValue);
137 |       }
138 |     }
139 | 
140 |     if (changed) {
141 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
142 |     }
143 | 
144 |     Set<String> unusedReplacements = dominantReplacements.keySet();
145 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(allDefines.keySet());
146 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(KNOWN_DEFINES);
147 |     for (String unknownDefine : unusedReplacements) {
148 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(UNKNOWN_DEFINE_WARNING, unknownDefine));
149 |     }
150 |   }

===== M0493 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

===== M0505 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 87-142
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
87 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
88 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
89 |     // just bail out early here.
90 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
91 |       return this;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
95 | 
96 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
97 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
98 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
99 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
100 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
101 |     }
102 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
103 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
104 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
105 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
106 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
107 |         }
108 |       } else {
109 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
110 |           return this;
111 |         }
112 | 
113 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
114 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
115 |         // one another.
116 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
117 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
118 |           JSType current = it.next();
119 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
120 |               current.isUnknownType()) {
121 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
122 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
123 |               return this;
124 |             }
125 |           } else {
126 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
127 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
128 |               return this;
129 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
130 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
131 |               it.remove();
132 |             }
133 |           }
134 |         }
135 |         alternates.add(alternate);
136 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
137 |       }
138 |     } else {
139 |       result = null;
140 |     }
141 |     return this;
142 |   }

     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */

474 | 
475 |         /*
476 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
477 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
478 |          * single 's.
479 |          */
480 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
481 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
482 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
483 |     }

===== M0517 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java

Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1022 |   Node parseInputs() {
1023 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1024 | 
1025 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1026 |     // individual file parse trees.
1027 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1028 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1029 |     }
1030 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1031 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1032 |     }
1033 | 
1034 |     // Parse main js sources.
1035 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1036 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1037 | 
1038 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1039 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1040 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1041 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1042 |     }
1043 | 
1044 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1045 | 
1046 |     try {
1047 |       // Parse externs sources.
1048 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1049 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1050 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1051 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1052 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1053 |           return null;
1054 |         }
1055 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1056 |       }
1057 | 
1058 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1059 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1060 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1061 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1062 | 
1063 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1064 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1065 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1066 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1067 |           }
1068 |         }
1069 | 
1070 |         try {
1071 |           inputs =
1072 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1073 |               .manageDependencies(inputs);
1074 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1075 |           report(JSError.make(
1076 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1077 |           return null;
1078 |         }
1079 |       }
1080 | 
1081 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1082 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1083 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1084 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1085 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1086 |           return null;
1087 |         }
1088 | 
1089 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1090 |         if (n == null) {
1091 |           continue;
1092 |         }
1093 | 
1094 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1095 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1096 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1097 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1098 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1099 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1100 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1101 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1102 | 
1103 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1104 | 
1105 |             externs.add(input);
1106 |             staleInputs = true;
1107 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1108 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1109 |             staleInputs = true;
1110 |           }
1111 |         }
1112 |       }
1113 | 
1114 |       if (staleInputs) {
1115 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1116 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1117 |       }
1118 | 
1119 |       // Build the AST.
1120 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1121 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1122 |         if (n == null) {
1123 |           continue;
1124 |         }
1125 | 
1126 |         if (devMode) {
1127 |           runSanityCheck();
1128 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1129 |             return null;
1130 |           }
1131 |         }
1132 | 
1133 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1134 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1135 | 
1136 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1137 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1138 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1139 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1140 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1141 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1142 |         }
1143 | 
1144 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1145 |       }
1146 | 
1147 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1148 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1149 | 
1150 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1151 |     } finally {
1152 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1153 |     }
1154 |   }
